6a2(1): shing 'tsho gzhes pa de dag thams cad kyang snang ngo //sangs rgyas ki zhing de dag gi sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag chos gang 'chad pa de dag thams cad la lus par grag go/sangs

6a4(1): kyi zhing de dag na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag mnyan pa dang /dmigs pa dang /mos pa rnam pa sna tshogs du ma'i rgyu'i thabs mkhas pa dag gis byang chub sems

6b1(1): gu|ci'i phyir bcom ldan 'das kyis ltas cho 'phrul chen po 'di lta bu 'di bstan te/bcom ldan 'das kyang ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs zhing /rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul chen po ngo 
6b2(1): mtshar rmad du byung ba chen po bsam gyis mi khyab pa 'di lta bu 'di dag kyang snang bar gyur pa 'di'i don bdag gis su la dri/'di na don lan ldon nus pa ni su yod snyam mo//de yang 'di snyam 
6b3(1): du 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa 'di sngon gyi rgyal ba rnams la lhag par bya ba byas pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa/sangs rgyas mang po la bsnyen bkur byas pa ste/'jam dpal 
6b4(1): gzhon nur gyur pa 'dis sngan chad kyang sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis ltas 'di lta bu byung ba rnams mthong zhing /sngan chad chos 
6b5(1): chen po'i 'brel pa'i gtam dag myong bas na bdag gis 'jam dpal gzhon nur 'gyur pa la/de'i don dri'o snyam du sems so//dge slong dang /dge slong ma dang /dge bsnyen dang /dge 
6b6(1): bsnyen ma'i 'khor bzhi po de dag dang /lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi dang mi ma yin pa mang 
6b7(1): po de dag kyang /bcom ldan 'das kyi ltas kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul chen po'i snang ba 'di lta bu 'di mthong nas ngo mtshar du gyur/rmad du gyur ltad mor gyur te/'di snyam du bcom ldan 'das 
8a1(1): mdzad//gzhan yang bde bar gshegs kyi sras po gang //bla na med pa'i ye shes tshol ba dag/bya ba sna tshogs rtag tu byed pa la//de dag la ni byang chub phyir 
8a2(1): bsngags gsung //'di na 'dug bzhin de na 'di 'dra dang //khyad par gzhan dag stong phrag bye ba dag/shin tu mthong zhing thos te 'jam pa'i dbyangs//de dag las ni phyogs tsam smos 
8a3(1): par bya//bdag mthong zhing rnams mang po de dag na'ang //byang chub sems dpa' gang+gA'i bye ma snyed//gang rnams bye ma stong phrag mi nyung ba//brtson 'grus rnam pa mang pos byang chub snyed//

8b1(1): las phangs pa'i sha yang byin par byed//byang chub dam pa 'di ni tshol ba'i phyir//lag pa dang ni rkang pa blangs na byin//la las bdag gi mgo dang la las mig/la las bdag
8b2(1): gi lus mchog dag kyang gtong //dang ba'i sems kyis sbyin pa rnams byin nas//de bzhin gshegs kyi ye shes don gnyer ba//'jam dpal bdag gis mthong ba la la ni//
8b3(1): rgyal srid skyid pa rnams ni spang byas te//btsun mo'i 'khor dang de bzhin gling kun dang //gnyen dang blon po thams cad rnam spangs nas//'jig rten rnam par 'dren gyi drung dong ste/zhi ba'i
8b4(1): phyir ni chos rab mchog 'dri zhing //ngur smig gos rnams rab tu gyon byed de//skra dang kha spu dag kyang 'dren par byed//bdag mthong byang chub sems dpa' la la ni//dge slong gyur
8b5(1): cing nags tshal gnas pa dag/dgon pa stong pa rnams su gnas 'cha' zhing //yongs su sems shing nye bar rtogs par byed//kha cig gis ni 'dod pa ma lus spangs//yongs

8b7(1): te//'dren pa'i spyan sngar thal mo sbyar byas nas//dga' ba bskyed de rgyal dbang rgyal po la//tshigs su bcad pa dag gis mngon par bstod//dran dang ldan zhing dul ba mi 'jigs pa/

26a1(1): @#//bu nga yang theg pa gcig la brtsams te/sems can rnams la chos ston pa 'di lta bu ste/sangs rgyas kyi theg pa thams cad mkhyen

26a3(1): de ci'i phyir zhe na/shA ri'i bu de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang dag 'das pa'i dus na/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams dpag

26b4(1): thams cad kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa' byang chub thob par gyur to//shA ri'i bu ma 'ongs pa'i dus na/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams dpag tu med grangs med

28a1(1): pha@#//pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nga yang sems can mos pa tha dad pa dang /khams dang bsam pa tha dad pa rnams kyi bsams pa mkhyen nas/skye bo

37a1(1): pha@#//'di lta bu gsung 'di/bcom ldan 'das las thos nas/bdag ngo mtshar dang rmad du gyur de dga' ba thob bo//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems
